# Summoner

---

You are the armada. The kings of old would trade armies for your abilities. You've undertaken the tradition that conjures an endless supply of warriors. You are the summoner, the mage who takes their dreams and makes them manifest.

You call forth minions to trudge fearlessly into the fray and provide support, holding the enemy at bay while you and your fellow heroes ready the counteroffensive. Your minions serve unflinchingly, unerringly, to their death or to yours.

You can also take advantage of powerful magic to buff your allies, whittle down your enemies, or enlist the fallen into your ranks. And when push comes to shove, you can call upon your champion to finish the fight.

## Basics {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/basics"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest" data-action="trait" data-sub="1">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>

</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>

<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span><b>Starting Characteristics:</b> You start with a <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> of 2, and you can choose one of the following arrays for your other <a href="../../rule/character/characteristic/">characteristics</a> scores:</p>
<ul><li>2, 2, -1, -1</li><li>2, 1, 1, -1</li><li>2, 1, 0, 0</li><li>1, 1, 1, 0</li></ul>
<p><b>Weak <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">Potency</a>:</b> <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> - 2</p>
<p><b>Average <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">Potency</a>:</b> <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> - 1</p>
<p><b>Strong <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">Potency</a>:</b> <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a></p>
<p><b>Starting <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> at 1st Level:</b> 15</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> Gained at 2nd and Higher Levels:</b> 6</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/health/recoveries/">Recoveries</a>:</b> 8</p>
<p><b>Skills:</b> You gain the <a href="../../skill/lore/magic/">Magic</a> and <a href="../../skill/lore/strategy/">Strategy</a> skills and can choose any two skills from the <a href="../../skill/intrigue/">intrigue</a> or <a href="../../skill/lore/">lore</a> skill groups. (<em>Quick Build:</em> <a href="../../skill/intrigue/eavesdrop/">Eavesdrop</a>, <a href="../../skill/lore/magic/">Magic</a>, <a href="../../skill/lore/monsters/">Monsters</a>, <a href="../../skill/lore/strategy/">Strategy</a>.)</p>

## Draw Steel Master Classes

The [summoner](summoner.md) is an example of a master class in Draw Steel. Master classes are intended for players who are familiar with the game and have played a few sessions already. They are not recommended for first time players—you need to know the rules before we start breaking them on you!

If you are new to Draw Steel, we recommend checking out The Delian Tomb starter adventure, which includes a step-by-step tutorial on how to play the game and breaks down all the working parts of a hero. Save Broadhurst first, and then you hurry back here!

## 1st-Level Features

As a [summoner](summoner.md), you gain the following features.

## Summoner Circle {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/summoner-circle"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Summoner Circle</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You&#39;ve learned the art of summoning from a member of a loose network of summoners called a circle. Each circle specializes in a distinct <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">portfolio</a> of creatures you can call into existence. Choose a summoner circle from the following options, each of which grants you a communication feature (<em>Quick Build:</em> Circle of Graves.)</p>
<p><b>Circle of Blight:</b> You are a demonologist who calls forth demons from the Abyssal Waste. Your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">portfolio</a> includes shapechanging demons that grow in hunger and power over time. You can communicate with creatures that have the Abyssal keyword even if you don&#39;t share a language.</p>
<p><b>Circle of Graves:</b> You are a necromancer who raises undead creatures from the Necropolitan Ruin. The corporeal and incorporeal creatures under your command are hardy and many. You can communicate with creatures that have the Undead keyword even if you don&#39;t share a language.</p>
<p><b>Circle of Spring:</b> You are a feybright who beckons pixies, nixies, and sprites from Arcadia. Your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">portfolio</a> features ephemeral fey spirits surrounded by weird and powerful magic. You can communicate with creatures that have the Fey keyword even if you don&#39;t share a language.</p>
<p><b>Circle of Storms:</b> You are a storm caster who summons elementals from Quintessence. This <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">portfolio</a> contains forces of nature that leave a big impact on the environment. You can communicate with creatures that have the Elemental keyword even if you don&#39;t share a language.</p>
<p>Your choice of circle decides the <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">portfolio</a> from which you summon minions, as well as many of the features you&#39;ll gain from this class. Your circle is your <a href="../../rule/general/subclass/">subclass</a>.</p>
</div>
</section>

## Minions {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/minions"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="2">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Minions</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>The creatures you control are called minions. You can summon and maintain up to a maximum of 8 minions. Your minions are considered allies at your level.</p>
<p>You can manage up to two squads of minions. Newly summoned minions can either be organized into a new squad or be distributed into other squads under your control. A squad can&#39;t contain more than eight minions, and all minions in the squad must have the same name.</p>
<p>The maximum <a href="../../rule/combat/distance/">distance</a> that you can summon minions and use specific conjuring abilities is called your Summoner&#39;s Range. Your Summoner&#39;s Range is equal to 5 + your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> score. You must have <a href="../../rule/combat/line-of-effect/">line of effect</a> to summon and give commands to minions within your Summoner&#39;s Range. Commanding a minion to take a <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">main action</a> or a <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">maneuver</a> while hidden reveals you.</p>
<p>You also have special minions at your disposal called signature minions, low-cost creatures that you&#39;ve become accustomed to summoning often. See <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">Portfolio</a> for more details about the types of minions you can summon.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Minions in Combat</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p><b>Start of Combat:</b> At the start of a combat encounter or some other stressful situation tracked in <a href="../../rule/combat/combat-round/">combat rounds</a> (as determined by the Director), you can summon up to two of your signature minions at no cost into unoccupied spaces within your Summoner&#39;s Range (no action required).</p>
<p><b>Start of <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">Turn</a>:</b> At the start of each of your turns during combat, you can summon up to three of your signature minions at no cost into unoccupied spaces within your Summoner&#39;s Range (no action required).</p>
<p><b>Summoning:</b> Each minion is summoned on the ground unless they can <a href="../../movement/fly/">fly</a> or <a href="../../movement/hover/">hover</a>. Unless an ability specifies, you can&#39;t summon any new minions beyond your minion maximum until the same number of existing minions are dismissed or destroyed.</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a>:</b> Minions in a squad pool their <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> together. Whenever a minion in a squad takes <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>, the squad&#39;s <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> pool is reduced by a number equal to the damage taken. Each time the pool is reduced by an amount equal to a single squad member&#39;s <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a>, one minion dies (starting with the minion that took damage, followed by the next nearest minion). If there is any excess damage after all minions in the squad are dead, you take damage equal to 2 + your level. Minions can&#39;t be <a href="../../rule/health/winded/">winded</a>, can&#39;t regain <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a>, and can&#39;t gain <a href="../../rule/health/temporary-stamina/">temporary Stamina</a>.</p>
<p><b>Area Effects:</b> The <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> from an area effect dealt to a squad&#39;s <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> pool can only kill up to the minions in its area. Any excess damage is ignored.</p>
<p><b>Strikes with Multiple Targets:</b> A squad&#39;s <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> pool only takes the largest single instance of <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> from a <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a> that targets two or more minions in that squad. Any additional effects still affect the minions targeted by the strike.</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/combat/condition/">Conditions</a>:</b> You resolve any <a href="../../rule/general/saving-throw/">saving throws</a> on <a href="../../rule/combat/condition/">conditions</a> affecting one or more of your minions. Treat <a href="../../rule/general/saving-throw/">saving throws</a> as if you had one instance of each condition.</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/damage/damage-immunity/">Damage Immunity</a> and <a href="../../rule/damage/damage-weakness/">Weakness</a>:</b> If any minion in a squad has <a href="../../rule/damage/damage-immunity/">damage immunity</a> or <a href="../../rule/damage/damage-weakness/">weakness</a> to a particular <a href="../../rule/damage/damage-type/">damage type</a>, apply that effect to the entire squad only once, regardless of how many minions share the same trait.</p>
<p><b>Actions:</b> Minions in a squad act together on your <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> in any order, before, in-between, and/or after any of your actions. They can either take a move action and a <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">main action</a> (excluding <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/heal/">Heal</a> and <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/defend/">Defend</a>), a move action and a <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">maneuver</a>, or two move actions. Individual minions can also make <a href="../../rule/combat/opportunity-attack/">opportunity attacks</a>.</p>
<p><b><a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">Free Strikes</a>:</b> Unless otherwise specified, a minion&#39;s <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> has a distance of Melee 1 or Ranged 5 and deals the damage value listed on the stat block. The minion can choose to deal untyped damage or the <a href="../../rule/damage/damage-type/">damage type</a> next to the damage value.</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">Damage</a>:</b> Whenever multiple minions <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a> the same target simultaneously, the damage is added together and treated as a single strike. Minions in a squad targeting the same target with a <a href="../../rule/combat/signature-ability/">signature ability</a> only apply one instance of the signature ability while each additional minion increases the damage by a number equal to their <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> value.</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">Surges</a>:</b> Your minions share your pool of <a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surges</a> and can apply them to their strikes. Whenever one or more of your minions would gain a <a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surge</a> during a <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, you gain that surge instead.</p>
<p><b>Maneuvers:</b> Unless otherwise specified on the minions&#39; stat block, a squad uses their maneuver together as a unit. If a maneuver targets a single creature, all minions in the squad target the same creature. If a maneuver requires a <a href="../../rule/dice/power-roll/">power roll</a>, the result is equal to 8 + the minions&#39; characteristic + the number of squad members within distance of the maneuver.</p>
<p><b>Individual Maneuvers:</b> An individual minion can use a maneuver to alleviate their own circumstances, such as getting up from <a href="../../condition/prone/">prone</a> or escaping a <a href="../../condition/grabbed/">grab</a>. If they do, they can&#39;t take part in their squad&#39;s main action or maneuver.</p>
<p><b><a href="../../rule/character/characteristic/">Characteristics</a>:</b> Your minions have their own <a href="../../rule/character/characteristic/">characteristics</a> for the purposes of resisting <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potencies</a>, maneuvers, and making tests. You use your own <a href="../../rule/character/characteristic/">characteristics</a> where a minion&#39;s stat block refers to an R or uses a <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potency</a> (such as M &lt; WEAK).</p>
<p><b>Unconscious:</b> If you are unconscious or unable to act on your <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, you can&#39;t summon new minions. Additionally, your remaining minions can&#39;t deal damage; they can only act to bring you to safety.</p>
<p><b>End of Combat:</b> At the end of combat, your minions finish their tasks (such as carrying someone to safety) and are then dismissed.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Minions Outside of Combat</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>While outside of combat, you can have up to 4 minions summoned without spending <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a>. You can freely summon your signature minions this way. For other minions, while you have a number of <a href="../../rule/resource/victories/">Victories</a> equal to the minion&#39;s essence cost or more, you can summon up to the set number of minions listed on their stat block.</p>
<p>Each of your minions can be given a simple task and a destination you&#39;ve previously visited and they&#39;ll fulfill it to the best of their ability. Example tasks include sending messages, scouting, and carrying supplies. Your minions aren&#39;t <a href="../../rule/general/follower/">followers</a> and can&#39;t make <a href="../../rule/downtime/project-roll/">project rolls</a> until you can summon specialists (see <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-6/minion-machinations/">Minion Machinations</a>).</p>
<p>When combat begins, any of your minions who were summoned outside of combat finish their tasks and are then dismissed.</p>
<p>&gt; <b>Soul-y Moley!</b> &gt; &gt; Your minions are manifestations of the magic you weave and don&#39;t harbor any souls of their own. They are expressions of your own soul and are more akin to dreams than sapient creatures. &gt; &gt; Treat your minions as if they had your soul whenever they are targeted by abilities that affect souls. Ignore any effects that trigger when a creature with a soul dies unless you&#39;re the one being targeted.</p>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Essence {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/essence"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="2">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Essence</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You and your minions have a unique reserve of essence as your <a href="../../rule/resource/heroic-resource/">Heroic Resource</a>. You use this magic to sculpt your forces and maintain control over the battlefield.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Essence in Combat</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>At the start of a combat encounter or some other stressful situation tracked in <a href="../../rule/combat/combat-round/">combat rounds</a> (as determined by the Director), you gain essence equal to your <a href="../../rule/resource/victories/">Victories</a>.</p>
<p>At the start of each of your <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turns</a> during combat, you gain 2 essence.</p>
<p>The first time each <a href="../../rule/combat/combat-round/">round</a> that any minion (either yours or an enemy) dies unwillingly within your Summoner&#39;s Range, you gain 1 essence.</p>
<p>Whenever you use a <a href="../../rule/general/heroic-ability/">heroic ability</a> or call forth a minion that costs essence, you can willingly sacrifice one or more of your minions within your Summoner&#39;s Range to reduce the cost by 1. You can&#39;t kill minions this way if they used a <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">main action</a> or maneuver during the <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>. You can sacrifice more minions than you would reduce the cost by.</p>
<p>You lose any remaining essence at the end of the encounter.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Essence Outside of Combat</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Though you can&#39;t gain essence outside of combat, you can use your <a href="../../rule/general/heroic-ability/">heroic abilities</a> and effects that cost essence without spending it. Whenever you use an ability or effect outside of combat that costs essence, you can&#39;t use that same ability or effect outside of combat again until you gain at least 1 <a href="../../rule/resource/victories/">Victory</a> or finish a <a href="../../rule/resource/respite/">respite</a>.</p>
<aside class="sc-callout" data-action="callout">
<div class="sc-callout__title"><span class="sc-callout__dia"></span>More Wielders of Quintessence</div>
<div class="sc-callout__body">
<p>Essence is the &#34;stuff of creation.&#34; Just as the elementalist uses essence to conjure fire, lightning, or warp space and time, the summoner uses essence to manifest skeletons, conjure pixies, and call forth demons from the Abyssal Waste.</p>
<p>There may be more magicians in the future that also rely on essence for their magic.</p>
</div>
</aside>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Summoner Strike {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/summoner-strike"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest" data-action="trait" data-sub="1" data-grant="the Summoner Strike main action">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Summoner Strike</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>You have the following ability, which replaces both your melee and ranged <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a>.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Summoner Strike</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">A sudden burst of energy erupts from your implement and shocks your foes&#39; nerves.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Melee</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Ranged</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Strike</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Melee 1 or Ranged 5</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">One creature or object</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>R <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>. If the target has R &lt; WEAK, they are <a href="../../condition/slowed/">slowed</a> (save ends).</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Special</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>This ability has the Charge keyword when it&#39;s used as a melee <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a>.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Strike For Me {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/strike-for-me"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="1" data-grant="the Strike for Me triggered action">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Strike For Me</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You have the following free <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">triggered action</a>.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="triggered">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">)</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Triggered Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Strike for Me</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">Your minions fight in your stead.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Ranged</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Each of your minions</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="pre">Power Roll +</span><span class="chars"><a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a></span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">Up to three targets each make a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a></span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">Up to five targets each make a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a></span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">Up to seven targets each make a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a></span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Trigger</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You use a <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">triggered action</a> to make a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> or use a <a href="../../rule/combat/signature-ability/">signature ability</a>.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Special</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>On a <a href="../../rule/dice/natural-19-20/">natural 19 or 20</a>, each target makes a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a>.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>Your minions act in place of you making a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> or using a <a href="../../rule/combat/signature-ability/">signature ability</a>. If you were granted the <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">triggered action</a> against a specific target, your minions must strike the same target. If the <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">triggered action</a> granted you a <a href="../../rule/combat/signature-ability/">signature ability</a>, you have an <a href="../../rule/dice/edge/">edge</a> on the <a href="../../rule/dice/power-roll/">power roll</a>.</p><p><b>Your Minions Deal the Damage</b></p><p>By normal Draw Steel rules, everyone gets access to <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a>. As a <a href="./">summoner</a>, you have a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> equivalent in addition to minions who have their own free strikes. Changing how you attack allows your minions to shine in battle while keeping your damage output in line with your fellow heroes. The static damage you and most of your minions deal also keeps your <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turns</a> fast and flowing.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Call Forth {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/call-forth"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest" data-action="trait" data-sub="1" data-grant="the Call Forth (1+ Essence) main action">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Call Forth</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>You have the following ability.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Call Forth (1+ Essence)</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">My power is yours, and yours, mine. I summon thee.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Ranged</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Self</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You summon one or more minions from your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">portfolio</a> into unoccupied spaces within distance. Choose one of the following options:</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Signature Minions</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You summon one <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">signature minion</a> for each essence you spend on this ability.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">All Other Minions</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You summon the set number of minions listed on the stat block for their essence cost.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Minion Bridge {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/minion-bridge"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="1" data-grant="the Minion Bridge maneuver">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Minion Bridge</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You have the following maneuver.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="maneuver">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">f</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Maneuver</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Minion Bridge</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">Your minions do everything in their power to form a safe path for you to cross.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Melee 1</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">One of your minions</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> into a square adjacent to the target, including vertically.</p><p>You can <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> into squares that contain one of your minions, even if they occupy <a href="../../movement/difficult-terrain/">difficult terrain</a>. Each time you <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> into a square that contains one of your minions while using this maneuver, you can <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> an additional square.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">1 Essence</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>An adjacent ally can <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> alongside you during this movement. They must end their movement in an unoccupied square adjacent to the last minion you moved through.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## 1st-Level Circle Features {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/1st-level-circle-features"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="9">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">1st-Level Circle Features</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Your circle grants you features as shown on the 1st-Level Summoner Circle Features table.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">1st-Level Summoner Circle Features</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<table><thead><tr><th>Circle</th><th>Feature</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Blight</td><td><a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/death-snap/">Death Snap</a>, <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/soulsense/">Soulsense</a></td></tr><tr><td>Graves</td><td><a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/dead-men-tell-all-tales/">Dead Men Tell All Tales</a>, <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/rise/">Rise!</a></td></tr><tr><td>Spring</td><td><a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/fairy-whispers/">Fairy Whispers</a>, <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/pixie-dust/">Pixie Dust</a></td></tr><tr><td>Storms</td><td><a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/elemental-affinity/">Elemental Affinity</a>, <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/heart-of-nature/">Heart of Nature</a></td></tr></tbody></table>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Dead Men Tell All Tales</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You can touch the corpse of a creature who died within the past week and ask them a question. The corpse can choose to answer the question to the best of their ability. Each additional question you ask the corpse requires a medium <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> <a href="../../rule/test/test/">test</a>, where failure or consequence breaks your connection with the corpse permanently.</p>
<p>The corpse can also choose to refuse to answer or lie, especially if you were the one to kill them in the first place.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Death Snap</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Whenever one of your demon minions would die unwillingly, they can deal <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to an <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> creature equal to their <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> value before dying.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Elemental Affinity</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Whenever you use <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/call-forth/">Call Forth</a> to summon one or more non-signature elemental minions, you can summon one bonus signature minion at no cost. You can choose between a signature minion that shares an Element keyword with the minions you summoned (such as Fire, Earth, or Air) or an elemental mote.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Fairy Whispers</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Whenever you send a minion to perform a task for you outside of combat, they can bring back a rumor from the destination to which you sent them. When the minion returns, make a <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> <a href="../../rule/test/test/">test</a>:</p>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">You learn an undoubtedly false common rumor.</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">You learn a common rumor that is most likely true.</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">You learn an obscure rumor that could either be true or false.</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<p>You gain a <a href="../../rule/dice/bane/">bane</a> on the test for each subsequent rumor you collect either on the same day or in the same location.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Heart of Nature</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You can sense the presence of creatures with the Elemental or Dragon keywords within 1 mile of you. You can innately feel their emotions or pain, and you can&#39;t obtain lower than a tier 2 outcome on any <a href="../../rule/character/intuition/">Intuition</a> <a href="../../rule/test/test/">test</a> made to socially interact with them.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Pixie Dust</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Increase your number of <a href="../../rule/health/recoveries/">Recoveries</a> by 2.</p>
<p>Whenever one of your fey minions dies within your Summoner&#39;s Range, you can spend a <a href="../../rule/health/recoveries/">Recovery</a> to give <a href="../../rule/health/temporary-stamina/">temporary Stamina</a> equal to twice your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> score to each non-minion ally <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the minion when they died.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Rise!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Once per <a href="../../rule/combat/combat-round/">round</a>, when a creature dies unwillingly within your Summoner&#39;s Range, you can use a <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">triggered action</a> to summon a signature undead minion in their space at no cost, even if you&#39;re at your minion maximum, but only if they can be organized into one of your squads. The new minion can&#39;t act until the start of your next <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>.</p>
<p>This ability becomes a free <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">triggered action</a> if the target was a minion (either yours or an enemy).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Soulsense</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>While you have <a href="../../rule/combat/line-of-effect/">line of effect</a> to a creature with a soul, you can perceive a trail of where the creature has been in the last number of minutes equal to 5 × your level.</p>
<p>When you finish a <a href="../../rule/resource/respite/">respite</a>, you can always perceive the soul trails of each creature that took the <a href="../../rule/resource/respite/">respite</a> with you until your next <a href="../../rule/resource/respite/">respite</a>.</p>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Formation {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/formation"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="4">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Formation</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You&#39;ve practiced a specific formation for your minions. Choose one of the following formations: horde, platoon, elite, or leader. You can change your formation along with your quick command (see <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/quick-command/">Quick Command</a>) by performing intense study as a <a href="../../rule/resource/respite/">respite</a> activity. (<em>Quick Build:</em> <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/platoon-formation/">Platoon Formation</a>.)</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Horde Formation</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Your maximum number of minions increases by 4 and you summon up to four of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">signature minions</a> at the start of each of your turns instead of three.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Platoon Formation</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Whenever one of your squads uses a damaging ability, choose one target of that ability to take additional <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> equal to your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> score.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Elite Formation</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Each of your minions have their <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> increased by 3 and their <a href="../../rule/character/stability/">stability</a> increased by 1.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Leader Formation</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You aren&#39;t affected by excess <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> after all minions in a squad are dead. If your minion is within your Summoner&#39;s Range when they take <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>, you can choose to take damage in place of the minion.</p>
<p>Additionally, you can use light armor <a href="../../rule/treasure/leveled-treasure/">treasures</a> and light weapon <a href="../../rule/treasure/leveled-treasure/">treasures</a> while you don&#39;t have a kit.</p>
<aside class="sc-callout" data-action="callout">
<div class="sc-callout__title"><span class="sc-callout__dia"></span>Minions and Treasures</div>
<div class="sc-callout__body">
<p><a href="../../rule/treasure/leveled-treasure/">Treasures</a> in Draw Steel are typically worded for you to use, which might create ambiguity for a <a href="./">summoner</a> managing an army. You can use the following guidelines to ensure the treasures you come across in your adventures do what they were intended to do.</p>
<ul><li>Treasures that give you a <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> bonus to <a href="../../rule/damage/rolled-damage/">rolled damage</a> only apply to <a href="../../rule/dice/power-roll/">power rolls</a> made from a non-minion&#39;s space. However, when you use <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/strike-for-me/">Strike for Me</a>, any bonus damage is applied to each enemy and object that took damage.</li><li>Once you reach level 4 as a <a href="./">summoner</a>, you regain access to your normal <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> abilities while wielding a weapon treasure, an <a href="../../rule/treasure/implement/">implement</a> treasure, or any treasure that enables you to make a <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> as a part of its effect.</li><li>Treasures that increase the <a href="../../rule/combat/distance/">distance</a> of ranged magic abilities also increase the distance of your Summoner&#39;s Range.</li></ul>
</div>
</aside>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Quick Command {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/quick-command"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest" data-action="trait" data-sub="8">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Quick Command</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>You have a special command you can issue to your minions. Choose one of the following quick commands. You can change your quick command along with your formation (see <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/formation/">Formation</a>) by performing intense study as a <a href="../../rule/resource/respite/">respite</a> activity. (<em>Quick Build:</em> <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/shield/">Shield!</a>)</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Focus Fire!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You have the following triggered action.</p>
</div>
</section>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="triggered">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">)</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Triggered Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Focus Fire!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">You ensure the enemy can&#39;t escape the incoming attack.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">—</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Self or one ally</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Trigger</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>The target deals <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to another creature.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>The target gains a <a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surge</a> for each of your minions <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to them (up to a maximum of 3 surges), which they can use on the triggering damage.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__enh"><span class="cost">Spend 1 Essence</span><span class="txt">If the triggering damage is from an ability that uses a <a href="../../rule/dice/power-roll/">power roll</a>, the <a href="../../rule/dice/power-roll/">power roll</a> gains an <a href="../../rule/dice/edge/">edge</a>.</span></div>
</article>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Halt!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You have the following triggered action.</p>
</div>
</section>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="triggered">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">)</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Triggered Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Halt!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">You order a minion to get in the way.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">—</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">One creature</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Trigger</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>The target starts their <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, moves, or is <a href="../../movement/forced-movement/">force moved</a>.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You summon a signature minion in an unoccupied space <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the target. If the target is <a href="../../movement/forced-movement/">force moved</a> into the minion, you can choose to make the target take no <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> from the collision.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Special</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>Instead of summoning a new minion, you can command one of your minions within <a href="../../rule/combat/distance/">distance</a> to <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> up to their <a href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a> toward a square <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the target before any additional effects occur.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Not Yet!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You have the following triggered action.</p>
</div>
</section>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="triggered">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">)</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Triggered Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Not Yet!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">I command you to not die.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">—</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">One ally</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Trigger</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>The target receives enough damage to die or be destroyed.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Special</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>If the target is a minion, they must be the only minion remaining in their squad.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>The <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> the target receives is reduced by an amount that leaves the target alive with 1 point of <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a>.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Shield!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You have the following triggered action.</p>
</div>
</section>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="triggered">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">)</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Triggered Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Shield!</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">You call upon a minion to use their body to dampen the blow.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">—</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Self or one ally</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Trigger</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>The target is targeted by a <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a>.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>If one of your minions is <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the target and is within <a href="../../rule/combat/distance/">distance</a> of the strike, they become the new target of the strike.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__enh"><span class="cost">Spend 1 Essence</span><span class="txt">Instead of commanding an existing minion, you summon a signature minion into an unoccupied space <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the target to take the strike.</span></div>
</article>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## Portfolio {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/portfolio"}

Your portfolio comprises the minions you've learned to summon. Each summoner circle specializes in a portfolio of creatures with which the summoner can build their armies, as shown on the Summoner Circle Portfolio table. Your circle allows you to select minions to add to your own portfolio.

##### Summoner Circle Portfolio

| Circle | Portfolio |
|--------|-----------|
| Blight | Demon Portfolio |
| Graves | Undead Portfolio |
| Spring | Fey Portfolio |
| Storms | Elemental Portfolio |

#### Demon Portfolio Signature Minion

Select two of the following [signature minions](../feature/summoner/level-1/minions.md). Signature minions are summoned at the start of combat and at the start of your turn for free. You can also use Call Forth to summon any number of signature minions for their essence cost.

##### Ensnarer {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.demon.statblock/ensnarer"}

<div class="sb-wrap" data-role="brute" data-creature="ensnarer"><div class="sb__sticky" aria-hidden="true"><div class="sb__sticky-row1"><span class="sb__sticky-id"><span class="sb__sticky-name">Ensnarer</span><span class="sb__sticky-role" data-role="brute">Minion Brute</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-stats"><span class="sb__sticky-defs"><span class="m"><b>1M</b>Size</span><span class="m"><b>5</b>Speed</span><span class="m"><b>2</b>Stamina</span><span class="m"><b>0</b>Stability</span><span class="m"><b>2</b>Free Strike</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-chars"><span class="c"><b>+2</b><i>M</i></span><span class="c"><b>+0</b><i>A</i></span><span class="c"><b>-1</b><i>R</i></span><span class="c"><b>-1</b><i>I</i></span><span class="c"><b>-1</b><i>P</i></span></span></span></div><div class="sb__sticky-row2"><span class="sm"><b>Movement</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>With Captain</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Immunity</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Weakness</b>Holy 1</span></div></div><article class="sb md-typeset" data-role="brute"><header class="sb__head"><div class="sb__head-row"><div class="sb__identity"><div class="sb__kw">Summoner Minion · Demon</div><h2 class="sb__name">Ensnarer</h2></div><div class="sb__class"><div class="sb__role" data-role="brute">Minion Brute</div><div class="sb__ev sb__cost">1 essence per minion summoned</div></div></div></header><div class="sb__flavor">This vaguely humanoid form is warped and distorted by a demon nestled inside them. class="sb__char-v">-1</span><span class="sb__char-l">Presence</span></div></div><div class="sb__features"><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Extended Barbed Strike</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The ensnarer's melee <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> have a distance of 3 and inflict <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">pull</a> 1. The <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">pull</a> distance increases by 1 for each additional ensnarer striking the same target. Choose the ensnarer that the target is being pulled to before applying <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">forced movement</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Soulsight</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each creature adjacent to the ensnarer can't be <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/maneuvers/hide/">hidden</a> from them.</p></article></div></article></div>

##### Rasquine {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.demon.statblock/rasquine"}

<div class="sb-wrap" data-role="ambusher" data-creature="rasquine"><div class="sb__sticky" aria-hidden="true"><div class="sb__sticky-row1"><span class="sb__sticky-id"><span class="sb__sticky-name">Rasquine</span><span class="sb__sticky-role" data-role="ambusher">Minion Ambusher</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-stats"><span class="sb__sticky-defs"><span class="m"><b>1S</b>Size</span><span class="m"><b>4</b>Speed</span><span class="m"><b>2</b>Stamina</span><span class="m"><b>0</b>Stability</span><span class="m"><b>2</b>Free Strike</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-chars"><span class="c"><b>-1</b><i>M</i></span><span class="c"><b>+0</b><i>A</i></span><span class="c"><b>-1</b><i>R</i></span><span class="c"><b>-1</b><i>I</i></span><span class="c"><b>+2</b><i>P</i></span></span></span></div><div class="sb__sticky-row2"><span class="sm"><b>Movement</b>Teleport</span><span class="sm"><b>With Captain</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Immunity</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Weakness</b>Holy 1</span></div></div><article class="sb md-typeset" data-role="ambusher"><header class="sb__head"><div class="sb__head-row"><div class="sb__identity"><div class="sb__kw">Summoner Minion · Demon</div><h2 class="sb__name">Rasquine</h2></div><div class="sb__class"><div class="sb__role" data-role="ambusher">Minion Ambusher</div><div class="sb__ev sb__cost">1 essence per minion summoned</div></div></div></header><div class="sb__flavor">The rasquine are skulking demons that shimmer in the light. They teleport into position before biting the necks of their prey.</div><div class="sb__defenses"><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">1S</span><span class="l">Size</span></div><div class="sb__char-v">+2</span><span class="sb__char-l">Presence</span></div></div><div class="sb__features"><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Skulker</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Once per turn, the rasquine can <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/maneuvers/hide/">hide</a> as a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/free-maneuver/">free maneuver</a> after <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/teleport/">teleporting</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" #### Demon Portfolio 3-Essence Minion

Choose two of the following minions. You can use Call Forth to summon the listed number of 3-Essence minions for their essence cost.

class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The musilex's melee <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> have a distance of 2 + R and inflict <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">pull</a> 2. The <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">pull</a> distance increases by 2 for each additional musilex striking the same target. Choose the musilex that the target is being pulled to before applying <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">forced movement</a>. If the target is pulled adjacent to the musilex, the musilex either deals an additional 2 damage or <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/maneuvers/grab/">grabs</a> them.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Soulsight</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each creature adjacent to the musilex can't be <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/maneuvers/hide/">hidden</a> from them.</p></article></div></article></div>

#### Elemental Portfolio Signature Minion

You gain the elemental mote and can choose one of the following [signature minions](../feature/summoner/level-1/minions.md). Signature minions are summoned at the start of combat and at the start of your turn for free. You can also use Call Forth to summon any number of signature minions for their essence cost.

##### Elemental Mote {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.elemental.statblock/elemental-mote"}

sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Dweomer Burst</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">When the mote is reduced to 0 <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a>, each enemy adjacent to the mote has a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/dice/bane/">bane</a> on their next <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Catalyst</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Once per turn, the mote can transform into an adjacent allied <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">signature minion</a>, maintaining their current Stamina. The minion must be reassigned to a new squad if their new name differs from the other squad members.

Alternatively, you can spend 1 essence to transform the mote into any <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">signature minion</a> in the elemental portfolio you don't have, as if you summoned the new minion into the mote's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

#### Elemental Portfolio 3-Essence Minion

Choose two of the following minions. You can use Call Forth to summon the listed number of 3-Essence minions for their essence cost.

class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The crux's melee <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> M &lt; AVERAGE automatically <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/maneuvers/hide/">hide</a> each ally from the target until the start of the crux's next turn, until the target uses a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/turn/">maneuver</a> to clear the soot, until the crux takes damage, or until the crux is destroyed.</p></article></div></article></div>

&lt; AVERAGE <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/slowed/">slowed</a> (save ends). #### Fey Portfolio Signature Minion

Select two of the following [signature minions](../feature/summoner/level-1/minions.md). Signature minions are summoned at the start of combat and at the start of your turn for free. You can also use Call Forth to summon any number of signature minions for their essence cost.

class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Once per turn during their move action, each nixie under your control can <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/teleport/">teleport</a> to a body of water within 5. The soakreed can't <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/teleport/">teleport</a> into water created by their own soaking bog.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Soaking Bog</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The area within 1 square of the soakreed is filled with swampy water. An enemy that starts their turn in the area is A &lt; WEAK <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/slowed/">slowed</a> (<a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/end-of-turn/">EoT</a>). The <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/character/potency/">potency</a> increases by 1 for each additional soaking bog the target occupies (maximum +2).</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The soakreed has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Fairy Chime</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each ally within 1 square of a bellringer has a +1 to <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/general/saving-throw/">saving throws</a>. Each enemy within 1 square of a bellringer has a -1 to <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/general/saving-throw/">saving throws</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The bellringer has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

##### Sprite Dandeknight {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.fey.statblock/sprite-dandeknight"}

They're usually clad in tassels that shift color as they swing their weapons.</div><div class="sb__defenses"><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">1T</span><span class="l">Size</span></div><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Magic Strike</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">When the dandeknight strikes, you can choose one of the following damage types: acid, cold, corruption, fire, lightning, poison, or sonic. The strike deals that damage.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Staccato Swings</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">When the dandeknight makes a <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a>, they can make two <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> instead. The damage is added together and treated as a single <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a> if both strikes hit the same target.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The dandeknight has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

#### Fey Portfolio 3-Essence Minion

Choose two of the following minions. You can use Call Forth to summon the listed number of 3-Essence minions for their essence cost.

class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Floating Toxins</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The area within 1 square of the loftlilly causes each enemy and object with a size equal to your <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> or smaller to float 1 square off the ground until they leave the area. A floating enemy that can't <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/fly/">fly</a> is unable to <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a>, moves 2 additional squares from <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">forced movement</a>, and has a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/dice/bane/">bane</a> on <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strikes</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The loftlilly has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Fairy Guard</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each non-orchiguard ally takes half damage from abilities while within 1 square of the orchiguard. Whenever the orchiguard reduces damage this way, they take damage equal to half their maximum <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> and their <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> damage increases by 1.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The orchiguard has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

#### Undead Portfolio Signature Minion

Select two of the following [signature minions](../feature/summoner/level-1/minions.md). Signature minions are summoned at the start of combat and at the start of your turn for free. You can also use Call Forth to summon any number of signature minions for their essence cost. (Quick Build: Skeleton, Husk.)

##### Husk {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.undead.statblock/husk"}

#### Undead Portfolio 3-Essence Minion

Choose two of the following minions. You can use Call Forth to summon the listed number of 3-Essence minions for their essence cost. (Quick Build: Stalker Shade, Grave Knight.)
