class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> before being destroyed.</p></article></div></article></div>

##### Stalker Shade {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.undead.statblock/stalker-shade"}

<div class="sb-wrap" data-role="ambusher" data-creature="stalker-shade"><div class="sb__sticky" aria-hidden="true"><div class="sb__sticky-row1"><span class="sb__sticky-id"><span class="sb__sticky-name">Stalker Shade</span><span class="sb__sticky-role" data-role="ambusher">Minion Ambusher</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-stats"><span class="sb__sticky-defs"><span class="m"><b>1M</b>Size</span><span class="m"><b>5</b>Speed</span><span class="m"><b>6 | 6</b>Stamina</span><span class="m"><b>1</b>Stability</span><span class="m"><b>5</b>Free Strike</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-chars"><span class="c"><b>-2</b><i>M</i></span><span class="c"><b>+1</b><i>A</i></span><span class="c"><b>+0</b><i>R</i></span><span class="c"><b>+0</b><i>I</i></span><span class="c"><b>+2</b><i>P</i></span></span></span></div><div class="sb__sticky-row2"><span class="sm"><b>Movement</b>Fly, hover</span><span class="sm"><b>With Captain</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Immunity</b>Corruption R, Poison R</span><span class="sm"><b>Weakness</b>—</span></div></div><article class="sb md-typeset" data-role="ambusher"><header class="sb__head"><div class="sb__head-row"><div class="sb__identity"><div class="sb__kw">Summoner Minion · Undead</div><h2 class="sb__name">Stalker Shade</h2></div><div class="sb__class"><div class="sb__role" data-role="ambusher">Minion Ambusher</div><div class="sb__ev sb__cost">3 essence for two minions</div></div></div></header><div class="sb__flavor">Shades are a form of umbral stalker that float free from any floor or surface. They can bend their appearance to completely vanish in the light.</div><div class="sb__defenses"><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">1M</span><span class="l">Size</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">5</span><span class="l">Speed</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">6 | 6</span><span class="l">Stamina</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">1</span><span class="l">Stability</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">5</span><span class="l">Free Strike</span></div></div><div class="sb__meta"><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">Immunity</span><span class="sb__field-v">Corruption R, Poison R</span></div><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">Weakness</span><span class="sb__field-v">—</span></div><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">Movement</span><span class="sb__field-v">Fly, hover</span></div><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">With Captain</span><span class="sb__field-v">—</span></div></div><div class="sb__chars"><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">M</span><span class="sb__char-v">-2</span><span class="sb__char-l">Might</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">A</span><span class="sb__char-v">+1</span><span class="sb__char-l">Agility</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">R</span><span class="sb__char-v">+0</span><span class="sb__char-l">Reason</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">I</span><span class="sb__char-v">+0</span><span class="sb__char-l">Intuition</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">P</span><span class="sb__char-v">+2</span><span class="sb__char-l">Presence</span></div></div><div class="sb__features"><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Shadow Strike</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The stalker shade turns invisible, <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/shifting/">shifts</a> 3 squares, and reappears after making a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Shadow Phasing</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The stalker shade can move through other creatures and objects at normal speed. The first time in a round that the stalker shade passes through a creature, that creature takes 2 corruption damage. The stalker shade doesn't take damage from being <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">force moved</a> into objects.</p></article></div></article></div>

##### Zombie Lumberer {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.undead.statblock/zombie-lumberer"}

<div class="sb-wrap" data-role="defender" data-creature="zombie-lumberer"><div class="sb__sticky" aria-hidden="true"><div class="sb__sticky-row1"><span class="sb__sticky-id"><span class="sb__sticky-name">Zombie Lumberer</span><span class="sb__sticky-role" data-role="defender">Minion Defender</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-stats"><span class="sb__sticky-defs"><span class="m"><b>2</b>Size</span><span class="m"><b>5</b>Speed</span><span class="m"><b>8 | 8</b>Stamina</span><span class="m"><b>—</b>Stability</span><span class="m"><b>1</b>Free Strike</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-chars"><span class="c"><b>+2</b><i>M</i></span><span class="c"><b>-2</b><i>A</i></span><span class="c"><b>+0</b><i>R</i></span><span class="c"><b>+0</b><i>I</i></span><span class="c"><b>+1</b><i>P</i></span></span></span></div><div class="sb__sticky-row2"><span class="sm"><b>Movement</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>With Captain</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Immunity</b>Corruption R, Poison R</span><span class="sm"><b>Weakness</b>—</span></div></div><article class="sb md-typeset" data-role="defender"><header class="sb__head"><div class="sb__head-row"><div class="sb__identity"><div class="sb__kw">Summoner Minion · Undead</div><h2 class="sb__name">Zombie Lumberer</h2></div><div class="sb__class"><div class="sb__role" data-role="defender">Minion Defender</div><div class="sb__ev sb__cost">3 essence for two minions</div></div></div></header><div class="sb__flavor">These massive, animated ogre corpses still maintain their incredible grip strength. When a lumberer falls, they'll take anything within reach down with them.</div><div class="sb__defenses"><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">2</span><span class="l">Size</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">5</span><span class="l">Speed</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">8 | 8</span><span class="l">Stamina</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">—</span><span class="l">Stability</span></div><div class="sb__stat"><span class="v">1</span><span class="l">Free Strike</span></div></div><div class="sb__meta"><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">Immunity</span><span class="sb__field-v">Corruption R, Poison R</span></div><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">Weakness</span><span class="sb__field-v">—</span></div><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">Movement</span><span class="sb__field-v">—</span></div><div class="sb__field sb__field--meta"><span class="sb__field-l">With Captain</span><span class="sb__field-v">—</span></div></div><div class="sb__chars"><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">M</span><span class="sb__char-v">+2</span><span class="sb__char-l">Might</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">A</span><span class="sb__char-v">-2</span><span class="sb__char-l">Agility</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">R</span><span class="sb__char-v">+0</span><span class="sb__char-l">Reason</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">I</span><span class="sb__char-v">+0</span><span class="sb__char-l">Intuition</span></div><div class="sb__char"><span class="sb__char-box">P</span><span class="sb__char-v">+1</span><span class="sb__char-l">Presence</span></div></div><div class="sb__features"><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Zombie Clutch</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The lumberer's melee <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> inflict A &lt; AVERAGE <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/grabbed/">grabbed</a>. A creature or object that starts their turn <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/grabbed/">grabbed</a> by the lumberer takes corruption damage equal to your <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Death Grasp</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">When the lumberer is reduced to 0 <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a>, they can latch onto an adjacent enemy before being destroyed. The enemy is M &lt; STRONG <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/restrained/">restrained</a> (<a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/end-of-turn/">EoT</a>).</p></article></div></article></div>

## Summoner Abilities {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-1/summoner-abilities"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="1">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Summoner Abilities</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">1</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Your own abilities focus on support and enable you to position and outfit your allies for success.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Heroic Abilities</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Breaking down your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a> into pure power can lead to devastating effects. Your <a href="../../rule/general/heroic-ability/">heroic abilities</a> enable you to punctuate your army&#39;s efforts.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">5-Essence Ability</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Choose one <a href="../../rule/general/heroic-ability/">heroic ability</a> from the following options, each of which costs 5 <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a> to use. (Quick Build: <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/rallying-cry/">Rallying Cry</a>.)</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Essence Transfer</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">You pierce your foe and repurpose some of that &#39;fiber of their being&#39; they weren&#39;t using.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Melee</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Strike</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Melee 1</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">One creature</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="pre">Power Roll +</span><span class="chars">Reason</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">5 + R corruption damage; 2 charges (see below)</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">8 + R corruption damage; 3 charges</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">11 + R corruption damage; 4 charges</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You can spend charges to activate one of the following effects. You can activate an effect multiple times. All charges disappear after using this ability.</p><ul><li>1 charge: You or an ally within your Summoner&#39;s Range can spend a <a href="../../rule/health/recoveries/">Recovery</a>.</li><li>1 charge: You or an ally within your Summoner&#39;s Range gain a <a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surge</a>.</li><li>2 charges: You call forth a signature <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> into an unoccupied space within your Summoner&#39;s Range.</li></ul></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Explosive Parade</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">Your minions swell with energy until they can no longer exist in this realm.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Ranged</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Special</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="pre">Power Roll +</span><span class="chars">Reason</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">You summon four <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">signature minions</a>.</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">You summon five <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">signature minions</a>.</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">You summon six <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">signature minions</a>.</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>The <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> are summoned within distance regardless of your minion maximum and without organizing them into squads. Each newly summoned <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> immediately moves up to their <a href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a> toward a creature or object.</p><p>If they move adjacent to their target, become targeted by an <a href="../../rule/combat/opportunity-attack/">opportunity attack</a>, or stop moving, they explode, dealing 2 <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to one adjacent creature or object and pushing them 1 square. If a target is affected by two or more minions&#39; explosions, the effects stack. These <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> activate no effects upon death, and you gain no <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a> from their deaths.</p></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Special</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>In addition to the <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> summoned as a part of this ability, you can choose to command any number of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> within distance, provided they haven&#39;t used a main action or maneuver during the <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="maneuver">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">f</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Maneuver</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Distraction Tactics</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">Your minions do the work to draw the heat away from your allies.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Self</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Special</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>Until the end of the encounter or until you are <a href="../../rule/health/dying/">dying</a>, each <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> under your control during the encounter is the target of the following effect:</p><p>The target&#39;s <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strikes</a> can inflict I &lt; WEAK <a href="../../condition/taunted/">taunted</a> (EoT) to enemies. The <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potency</a> increases by 1 for each <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> that joined the <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a>.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="maneuver">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">f</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Maneuver</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Rallying Cry</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">&#34;Show them what you&#39;re made of!&#34;</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Ranged</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">3 burst</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">All Allies</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>Each target chooses between gaining 2 <a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surges</a> or dealing additional <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> equal to your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> on their next <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a>.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="maneuver">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">f</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Maneuver</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Shields of Essence</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">You call forth protective forces to keep you all from harm.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Ranged</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Special</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="pre">Power Roll +</span><span class="chars">Reason</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">Three creatures</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">Four creatures</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">Five creatures</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>Until the end of the encounter, each target can use a <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">free triggered action</a> whenever they take <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to reduce the incoming <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> by half and then lose this effect.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Summoner&#39;s Sword</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">You draw your strength from the army you surround yourself with and summon a hot blade of energy and fervor.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Melee</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Strike</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Melee 3</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">One creature or object</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="pre">Power Roll +</span><span class="chars">Reason</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">R damage</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">2 + R damage</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">4 + R damage</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>This <a href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a> deals an additional 2 <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> for each ally <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to you.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
</div>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## 2nd-Level Features

As a 2nd-level [summoner](summoner.md), you gain the following features.

### Perk {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-2/perk"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Perk</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">2</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You gain an <a href="../../skill/intrigue/">intrigue</a>, <a href="../../skill/lore/">lore</a>, or supernatural <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-2/perk/">perk</a> of your choice. See Perks in Draw Steel: Heroes for more information.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Summoner's Dominion {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-2/summoners-dominion"}

Your circle allows you to call forth a piece of your [portfolio's](../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio.md) dominion like any [minion](../feature/summoner/level-1/minions.md).

Once per encounter, you can use a maneuver to summon a fixture from your [minions'](../feature/summoner/level-1/minions.md) native manifold or origin into an unoccupied space on the ground within your Summoner's Range. You can spend 1 [essence](../feature/summoner/level-1/essence.md) to relocate the fixture as a [free maneuver](../rule/combat/free-maneuver.md) on your [turn](../rule/combat/turn.md). The fixture stays until the end of the encounter, until its [Stamina](../rule/health/stamina.md) is reduced to 0, or until you become [dying](../rule/health/dying.md).

Your fixture gains additional features at 5th and 9th level.

#### Demon Portfolio Fixture

The boil arises from the chaotic depths of the Abyssal Waste, concentrated into a heaving mass by the pressure of a coherent reality. As it slushes and threatens to burst, the noises drive nearby demons into a frenzy.

##### The Boil {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.fixture.demon.featureblock/the-boil"}

<div class="fb-wrap" data-role="support">
<article class="fb md-typeset">
<header class="fb__head">
<div class="fb__eyebrow">Hazard · Support</div>
<h2 class="fb__name">The Boil</h2>
</header>
<div class="fb__stats">
<div class="fb__stat"><div class="fb__stat-l">Stamina</div><div class="fb__stat-v">20 + your level</div></div>
<div class="fb__stat"><div class="fb__stat-l">Size</div><div class="fb__stat-v">2</div></div>
</div>
<div class="fb__feats">
<article class="sc-ability fb__feat" data-action="passive">
<div class="fb__feat-head"><span class="fb__feat-icon">⭐️</span><div class="fb__feat-titles"><h3 class="fb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Hunger Thrush</h3></div><div class="fb__feat-corner"></div></div>
<div class="fb__feat-body">Each enemy that starts their <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> within 3 squares of the boil is I &lt; AVERAGE <a href="../../condition/taunted/">taunted</a> (EoT) by the boil, or I &lt; WEAK <a href="../../condition/taunted/">taunted</a> (EoT) by the boil and can&#39;t move further from it.</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability fb__feat" data-action="passive">
<div class="fb__feat-head"><span class="fb__feat-icon">⭐️</span><div class="fb__feat-titles"><h3 class="fb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Oh, It Pops</h3></div><div class="fb__feat-corner"></div></div>
<div class="fb__feat-body">When the boil is destroyed, each enemy within 3 squares of the boil takes acid <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> equal to your level and is A &lt; STRONG <a href="../../condition/weakened/">weakened</a> (save ends).</div>
</article>
</div>
</article>
</div>

##### The Boil Advancement Features {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.fixture.demon.advancement-features/the-boil"}

<div class="fb__feat-body">You gain a <a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surge</a> the first time in a <a href="../../rule/combat/combat-round/">round</a> that your demon <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> deal 3 or more <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to a creature while you have <a href="../../rule/combat/line-of-effect/">line of effect</a> to the boil. You can choose to give the <a href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surge</a> to an ally who also has <a href="../../rule/combat/line-of-effect/">line of effect</a> to the boil.</div>

The vibrant waters of Arcadia pour through a hole in reality and pool into a verdant cup of paradise. As the pond babbles, it causes the surrounding flora to grow and provides the fey places to hide.

class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Gushing Strike</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The spewler's ranged <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> have a distance of 10 and <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">slides</a> the target R + 2 squares.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Spew Slide</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each time the spewler takes <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>, the spewler <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/shifting/">shifts</a> 2 after all effects resolve. Each square they exit during this movement is covered in slime until the end of the encounter. An enemy has a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/dice/bane/">bane</a> on <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strikes</a> while occupying a slimed square.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Soulsight</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each creature <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the spewler can't be hidden from them.</p></article></div></article></div>

additional 2 <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to each <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> enemy from whom they were hidden. The violent loses their disguise after striking.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Mimicry</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The violent uses the <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/maneuvers/hide/">Hide</a> maneuver at the start of their <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> as a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/free-maneuver/">free maneuver</a>, disguising themselves as a a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/character/size/">size</a> 1M or smaller object.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Soulsight</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each creature <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the violent can't be hidden from them.</p></article></div></article></div>

class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Encroaching Strike</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The principle's melee <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> have a distance of R and inflict M &lt; STRONG <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/grabbed/">grabbed</a>. The principle can have an unlimited number of creatures or objects <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/grabbed/">grabbed</a>. A creature <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/grabbed/">grabbed</a> by this <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/strike/">strike</a> still has their normal <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a>, but can't move farther away from the principle.</p></article></div></article></div>

##### Quiet of Snow {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.elemental.statblock/quiet-of-snow"}

data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span Surge</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">When the quiet is reduced to 0 <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a>, they launch a refreshing blast of air into an area equal to their <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/character/size/">size</a> + 1 within 5 before being destroyed. Each ally in the affected area gains a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/resource/surge/">surge</a>.</p></article></div></article></div>

#### Fey Portfolio 5-Essence Minion

Choose one of the following minions.

##### Nixie Hemloche {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.fey.statblock/nixie-hemloche"}

<div class="sb-wrap" data-role="hexer" data-creature="nixie-hemloche"><div class="sb__sticky" aria-hidden="true"><div class="sb__sticky-row1"><span class="sb__sticky-id"><span class="sb__sticky-name">Nixie Hemloche</span><span class="sb__sticky-role" data-role="hexer">Minion Hexer</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-stats"><span class="sb__sticky-defs"><span class="m"><b>1T</b>Size</span><span class="m"><b>6</b>Speed</span><span class="m"><b>4 | 4 | 4</b>Stamina</span><span class="m"><b>0</b>Stability</span><span class="m"><b>3</b>Free Strike</span></span><span class="sb__sticky-chars"><span class="c"><b>-2</b><i>M</i></span><span class="c"><b>+0</b><i>A</i></span><span class="c"><b>+1</b><i>R</i></span><span class="c"><b>+3</b><i>I</i></span><span class="c"><b>+2</b><i>P</i></span></span></span></div><div class="sb__sticky-row2"><span class="sm"><b>Movement</b>Swim</span><span class="sm"><b>With Captain</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Immunity</b>—</span><span class="sm"><b>Weakness</b>—</span></div></div><article class="sb md-typeset" data-role="hexer"><header class="sb__head"><div class="sb__head-row"><div class="sb__identity"><div class="sb__kw">Summoner Minion · Fey</div><h2 class="sb__name">Nixie Hemloche</h2></div><div class="sb__class"><div class="sb__role" data-role="hexer">Minion Hexer</div><div class="sb__ev sb__cost">5 essence for three minions</div></div></div></header><div class="sb__flavor">Hemloches are spotted nixies whose long, wavy hair endlessly bobs and flows into the water surrounding them. data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Water Weird</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Once per <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> during their move action, each nixie under your control can <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/teleport/">teleport</a> to a body of water within 6. The hemloche can't <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/teleport/">teleport</a> into water created by their own whirling waves.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Whirling Waves</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The area within 1 square of the hemloche is filled with churning water and is considered <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/difficult-terrain/">difficult terrain</a>. At the end of the hemloche's <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, the hemloche can choose to <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">slide</a> each enemy in the affected area 3 squares. An enemy that takes <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> while being <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/forced-movement/">force moved</a> is also M &lt; AVERAGE knocked <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/prone/">prone</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The hemloche has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

Each enemy has a <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/dice/bane/">bane</a> on <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/test/test/">tests</a> made to search for the foxglow and allies hidden in the affected area.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The foxglow has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>

## 3rd-Level Features

As a 3rd-level [summoner](summoner.md), you gain the following features.

### Summoner's Kit {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-3/summoners-kit"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest" data-action="trait" data-sub="4">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Summoner&#39;s Kit</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">3</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>You conjure a kit for yourself. This kit includes an <a href="../../rule/treasure/implement/">implement</a>, such as a rod or a baton, which grants you the following benefits:</p>
<ul><li>The <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> of your <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/summoner-strike/">Summoner Strike</a> ability increases to twice your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> score.</li><li>The <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potency</a> of your <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/summoner-strike/">Summoner Strike</a> ability increases to R &lt; AVERAGE.</li><li>The distance of your <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/summoner-strike/">Summoner Strike</a> ability is now equal to your Summoner&#39;s Range.</li></ul>
<p>Your kit also comes with wards like magic armor and transient <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> forces. Choose one of the following wards.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Conjured Ward</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">3</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You are clad in the natural defenses of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio/">portfolio</a> (bones, fairy wood, stone, writhing flesh). You gain a +3 bonus to <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> and that bonus increases by 3 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Emergency Ward</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">3</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>The first time each <a href="../../rule/combat/combat-round/">round</a> you take <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>, you can use a <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">free triggered action</a> to <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> 1 after the triggering effect resolves and summon a signature <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> into the square you left (as long as there is enough space).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Howling Ward</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">3</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You create a 1-<a href="../../rule/combat/aura/">aura</a> vortex of slicing magic around you when you enter combat. An enemy that starts their <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to you takes <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> equal to your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a>.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Snare Ward</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">3</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Whenever an <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> creature deals <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to you, you can use a <a href="../../rule/combat/triggered-action/">free triggered action</a> to pull that creature toward one of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> within your Summoner&#39;s Range a number of squares equal to your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> score.</p>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>

### 7-Essence Ability {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-3/7-essence-ability"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest" data-action="trait" data-sub="4">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">7-Essence Ability</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">3</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Choose one <a href="../../rule/general/heroic-ability/">heroic ability</a> from the following options, each of which costs 7 <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a> to use.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="maneuver">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">f</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Maneuver</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Blitz Tactics</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">Rush &#39;em! CRUSH &#39;EM!</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Self</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Special</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>Until the end of the encounter or you are <a href="../../rule/health/dying/">dying</a>, each <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> under your control during the encounter is the target of the following effect. The first time on a <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> that the target moves through an enemy&#39;s space, the enemy can choose to <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> 1 square or be M &lt; WEAK (or M &lt; AVERAGE if the target is larger than the enemy) knocked <a href="../../condition/prone/">prone</a>. The <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potency</a> increases by 1 for each subsequent target that moves through the enemy&#39;s space during the same move action.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Cavalry Call</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">A lone squad appears to disrupt the enemy&#39;s plans and peels off their forces, one by one.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Special</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Effect</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You summon a temporary squad containing 6 of your signature <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> regardless of your minion maximum within distance. Whenever one of these <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> deals <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to an enemy, the enemy is R &lt; AVERAGE compelled to move 5 squares toward the source of the <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> (provoking <a href="../../rule/combat/opportunity-attack/">opportunity attacks</a>). The <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potency</a> increases by 1 for enemies targeted by two or more of these <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a>.</p><p>These <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> die at the end of your <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, activate no effects upon death, and you gain no <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a> from their deaths.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Essence Funnel</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">You rapidly summon and sacrifice minions in order to power a devastating blast of magic.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Area</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">10 × 1 line within 1</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">Each enemy and object in the area</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="pre">Power Roll +</span><span class="chars">Reason</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">5 <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>; <a href="../../movement/forced-movement/">push</a> 2</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">9 <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>; <a href="../../movement/forced-movement/">push</a> 4</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">12 <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>; <a href="../../movement/forced-movement/">push</a> 6</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__section">
<div class="sc-ability__section-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="tag">Special</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__section-body"><p>You can choose to kill any number of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> within your Summoner&#39;s Range as a part of this ability, provided they haven&#39;t used a main action or maneuver during the <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>. Each target takes an additional 1 <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>, plus 1 <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> for each <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> killed this way. These <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> activate no effects upon death, and you gain no <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a> from their deaths.</p></div>
</div>
</article>
<article class="sc-ability sc-fil" data-action="main">
<div class="sc-ability__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-ability__crest"><span class="sc-ability__glyph">l</span></span>
<div class="sc-ability__titles">
<div class="sc-ability__eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Main Action</div>
<h3 class="sc-ability__name">Lead By Example</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__corner"></div>
</div>
<p class="sc-ability__flavor">Your minions watch as your implement crackles with power, ready to slam unbelievable force into your foe.</p>
<div class="sc-ability__kw"><span class="sc-ability__chip">Magic</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Melee</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Ranged</span><span class="sc-ability__chip">Strike</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__rail">
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Distance</div><div class="v">Melee 1 or Summoner&#39;s Range</div></div>
<div class="sc-ability__cell"><div class="l">Targets</div><div class="v">One enemy or object</div></div>
</div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr">
<div class="sc-ability__pr-head"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span><span class="pre">Power Roll +</span><span class="chars">Reason</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__pr-rows">
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="low"><span class="badge">!</span><span class="res">8 + R <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>; R &lt; WEAK <a href="../../condition/dazed/">dazed</a> (save ends)</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="mid"><span class="badge">@</span><span class="res">12 + R <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>; R &lt; AVERAGE <a href="../../condition/dazed/">dazed</a> (save ends)</span></div>
<div class="sc-ability__tier" data-tier="high"><span class="badge">#</span><span class="res">16 + R <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>; R &lt; STRONG <a href="../../condition/dazed/">dazed</a> (save ends)</span></div>
</div>
</div>
</article>
</div>
</div>
</section>

## 4th-Level Features

As a 4th-level [summoner](summoner.md), you gain the following features.

### Characteristic Increase {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-4/characteristic-increase"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Characteristic Increase</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">4</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> score becomes 3. Additionally, you can increase one of your <a href="../../rule/character/characteristic/">characteristic</a> scores by 1, to a maximum score of 3.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Minion Improvement {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-4/minion-improvement"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait" data-sub="1">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Minion Improvement</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">4</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Your maximum number of <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> increases by 4.</p>
<p>You can increase each of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions&#39;</a> <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> as shown on the 4th-Level Minion Stamina Increase table. Additionally, each <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> that receives a <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> boost can treat their <a href="../../rule/character/characteristic/">characteristics</a> as one higher for the purposes of resisting <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potencies</a> (to a maximum value of 3).</p>
<p>These benefits are not reflected in the stat blocks of new <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> you acquire.</p>
<div class="sc-trait__nest">
<section class="sc-trait" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">4th-Level Minion Stamina Increase</h3>
</div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<table><thead><tr><th>Minion</th><th>Stamina Increase</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Signature Minion</td><td>Stamina +1</td></tr><tr><td>3-Essence Minion</td><td>Stamina +3</td></tr><tr><td>5-Essence Minion</td><td>Stamina +2</td></tr></tbody></table>
</div>
</section>
</div>
</div>
</section>

### Essence Salvage {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-4/essence-salvage"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Essence Salvage</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">4</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>The first time each <a href="../../rule/combat/combat-round/">combat round</a> that any <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> unwillingly dies within your Summoner&#39;s Range, you gain 2 <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/essence/">essence</a> instead of 1.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Minion Chain {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-4/minion-chain"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Minion Chain</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">4</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Whenever you use <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/minion-bridge/">Minion Bridge</a> as a maneuver, each of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> within your Summoner&#39;s Range can <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> up to their <a href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a> before the maneuver takes effect, as long as each <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> that <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shifts</a> ends their movement <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to another one of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a>.</p>
<p>Additionally, your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> can chain themselves together to function as a ladder or a swinging rope. When your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> move as a part of using <a href="../../feature/ability/summoner/level-1/minion-bridge/">Minion Bridge</a>, each <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> can use this movement to <a href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> into a position directly beneath another one of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a>, hoisting them and each other <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> they have hoisted, until they form a chain. The chain can then choose to fall across an unoccupied space and/or the topmost <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> <a href="../../feature/common/maneuvers/grab/">grabs</a> an object to keep the chain steady.</p>
<p>The chain lasts until the start of your next <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> or until the chain is no longer steady. The chain can also end when a <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> in the chain is destroyed or when you command your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> to let go as a <a href="../../rule/combat/free-maneuver/">free maneuver</a>. All <a href="../../rule/character/size/">size</a> 1 <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> count as one square when determining the chain&#39;s length.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Perk {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-4/perk"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Perk</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">4</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You gain any one <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-4/perk/">perk</a> of your choice. See Perks in Draw Steel: Heroes for more information.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Skill {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-4/skill"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Skill</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">4</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You gain one skill of your choice.</p>
</div>
</section>

## 5th-Level Features

As a 5th-level [summoner](summoner.md), you gain the following features.

## 5th-Level Circle Feature {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/5th-level-circle-feature"}

Your circle grants you a feature as shown on the 5th-Level Summoner Circle Features table.

##### 5th-Level Summoner Circle Features

| Circle | Feature |
|--------|---------|
| Blight | Shaping, Soul Flense |
| Graves | Channel, Dread March |
| Spring | Flash Powder, Pixie Lift |
| Storms | Nature Watch, Split |

### Channel {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/channel"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Channel</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You can spend 1 uninterrupted minute to perform a ritual and use your body as a host for a willing spirit of a creature who died in the area. While hosting the spirit, you have access to their memories of the 24 hours leading up to their death and any skills they knew in life. You can also magically change your appearance to look like them while they were alive.</p>
<p>You can attempt to stop channeling the spirit at any time. If the spirit is hostile to you or you&#39;ve hosted them for at least 1 hour, you must make a medium <a href="../../rule/character/presence/">Presence</a> <a href="../../rule/test/test/">test</a>. On success, the spirit leaves your body. On failure, you become fully possessed by a haunt; you have no access to your skills and you can&#39;t get above a tier 2 result on <a href="../../rule/dice/power-roll/">power rolls</a> until you exorcise the haunt either by completing the <a href="../../project/find-a-cure/">Find a Cure</a> downtime project in Draw Steel: Heroes or taking a <a href="../../rule/resource/respite/">respite</a> with an exorcist.</p>
<p>After you stop channeling their spirit, you can&#39;t use this feature to channel the same creature again.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Dread March {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/dread-march"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Dread March</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You and your undead <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> don&#39;t spend additional <a href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a> to move through <a href="../../movement/difficult-terrain/">difficult terrain</a>. If one or more of your undead <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> would die while using their move action, they can choose to not die until the end of your <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Flash Powder {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/flash-powder"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Flash Powder</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p class="sc-trait__leadin"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Each ally that gains <a href="../../rule/health/temporary-stamina/">temporary Stamina</a> from your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/pixie-dust/">Pixie Dust</a> feature also gains one of the following effects until the end of their next <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> (or for 10 minutes if used outside of combat):</p>
<ul><li><b>Flight:</b> Their <a href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a> gains the <a href="../../movement/fly/">Fly</a> keyword.</li><li><b>Vanish:</b> They become invisible.</li><li><b>Water Weird:</b> As a <a href="../../rule/combat/free-maneuver/">free maneuver</a> once per <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, they can <a href="../../movement/teleport/">teleport</a> to a body of water within 5 squares of them.</li><li><b>Panacea:</b> They can end one <a href="../../rule/combat/condition/">condition</a> affecting them or stand up.</li></ul>
</div>
</section>

### Nature Watch {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/nature-watch"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Nature Watch</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You can spend 1 uninterrupted minute each day to perform a ritual and summon a special elemental mote called a beacon to patrol the area. This mote telepathically communicates any hostile creatures, hazards, or traps within 20 squares of them to you no matter how far away you are. You know the number of nearby hazards and which direction they&#39;re in relative to where the beacon is, but not their exact position. You can have a number of beacons active equal to your level.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Pixie Lift {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/pixie-lift"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Pixie Lift</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Your <a href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a> gains the <a href="../../movement/fly/">Fly</a> and <a href="../../movement/hover/">Hover</a> keywords. You lose the <a href="../../movement/hover/">Hover</a> keyword from this feature while you are <a href="../../condition/dazed/">dazed</a>, <a href="../../rule/health/dying/">dying</a>, or you fly more than 1 square above the surface of the ground.</p>
<p>If your <a href="../../rule/character/speed/">speed</a> previously had the <a href="../../movement/fly/">Fly</a> keyword, you can now <a href="../../movement/fly/">fly</a> while sneaking an additional number of squares equal to your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a>.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Shaping {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/shaping"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Shaping</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>You can spend 1 uninterrupted minute to perform a ritual that causes one of your <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> to fold their shape and disguise themself to look like a duplicate of you, including speaking basic Caelian, allowing them to (potentially) freely move through civilization while completing their tasks. You can have a number of <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> disguised at the same time equal to your <a href="../../rule/character/reason/">Reason</a> score.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Split {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/split"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Split</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>Once during your <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, you can use a <a href="../../rule/combat/free-maneuver/">free maneuver</a> to deal <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> to one of your elemental <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> equal to half their maximum <a href="../../rule/health/stamina/">Stamina</a> in order to create one additional copy of that <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> in an <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> unoccupied space and add them to their squad, even if you&#39;re at your minion maximum. You can&#39;t use this feature if it would kill one or more of the <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> in the squad.</p>
</div>
</section>

### Soul Flense {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/soul-flense"}

<section class="sc-trait sc-trait--crest sc-trait--lead" data-action="trait">
<header class="sc-trait__head">
<span class="sc-crest sc-trait__crest"><span class="sc-trait__glyph">*</span></span>
<div class="sc-trait__titles">
<div class="sc-trait__eyebrow"><span class="sc-trait__dia"></span>Summoner Circle Feature</div>
<h3 class="sc-trait__name">Soul Flense</h3>
</div>
<div class="sc-trait__tag">Level <span class="num">5</span></div>
</header>
<div class="sc-trait__body">
<p>As a maneuver, you can command one or more of your demon <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> to each deal <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> equal to their <a href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> value to an <a href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> ally. This <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> can&#39;t be reduced. The ally then ends a <a href="../../rule/combat/condition/">condition</a> affecting them and confers it to the demon that attacked them.</p>
<p>Additionally, whenever one of your demon <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minions</a> Death Snaps, their target is P &lt; WEAK affected by a <a href="../../rule/combat/condition/">condition</a> the <a href="../../feature/summoner/level-1/minions/">minion</a> was suffering from. The <a href="../../rule/character/potency/">potency</a> increases by 1 on each subsequent Death Snap the target takes <a href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a> from in the same <a href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> (maximum +2).</p>
</div>
</section>

### New Portfolio Minion {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/feature.summoner.level-5/new-portfolio-minion"}

Your circle allows you to select new [minions](../feature/summoner/level-1/minions.md) to add to your [portfolio](../feature/summoner/level-1/portfolio.md).

#### Demon Portfolio 7-Essence Minion

Choose one of the following minions.

Each enemy they move through during a <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/charge/">charge</a> takes 3 <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/damage/damage/">damage</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Soulsight</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Each creature <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> to the gorrre can't be hidden from them.</p></article></div></article></div>

##### Vicisittante {data-scc="mcdm.summoner.v1/monster.minion.summoner.demon.statblock/vicisittante"}

Whenever they receive an effect that allows them to move or <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> outside of their move action, they share the effect with each <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/adjacent/">adjacent</a> reaver they were summoned with.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Bladed Strike</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The reaver's <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strikes</a> inflict M &lt; WEAK <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/bleeding/">bleeding</a> (save ends). Each time the reaver inflicts <a class="sb-term" href="../../condition/bleeding/">bleeding</a> on a creature, they can <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/shifting/">shift</a> 2 and make an additional <a class="sb-term" href="../../feature/common/main-actions/free-strike/">free strike</a> on a new target.</p></article></div></article></div>

class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Water Weird</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">Once per <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a> during their move action, each nixie under your control can <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/teleport/">teleport</a> to a body of water within 6. The corallia can't <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/teleport/">teleport</a> into water created by their own seafoam pool.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Seafoam Pool</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The area within 2 squares of the corallia is filled with purifying saltwater that disables the effects of <a class="sb-term" href="../../movement/difficult-terrain/">difficult terrain</a> created by enemies. At the end of the corallia's <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/turn/">turn</a>, the corallia can scrub you or an ally in the affected area and end one <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/condition/">condition</a>.</p></article><article class="sc-ability sb__feat" data-action="passive" data-kind="passive"><div class="sb__feat-head"><span class="sc-crest sb__feat-crest"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><span class="sb__feat-icon"><span class="sb__feat-glyph">*</span></span><div class="sb__feat-titles"><div class="sb__feat-eyebrow"><span class="sc-ability__dia"></span>Trait</div><h3 class="sb__feat-name sc-ability__name">Minuscule</h3></div><div class="sb__feat-corner"></div></div><p class="sb__feat-body">The coralia has <a class="sb-term" href="../../rule/combat/cover/">cover</a> while occupying a larger creature's space.</p></article></div></article></div>
